### [76] [A Privacy-Preserving Framework Using Remote Data Science for Inter-Institutional Student Retention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12845)
*John Fields,K M Sajjadul Islam,Ruchitha Thota,Victor Chen,Praveen Madiraju*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究基于PySyft平台开发了远程数据科学（RDS）框架，采用半气隙架构实现跨机构学生保留率协作预测。通过三所大学合作验证，在严格遵循FERPA合规的前提下，分类性能（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695）保持一致，并提出了一种优先保障隐私的Data-Type-Aware Templates合成数据方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育机构间共享敏感学生数据存在隐私合规（如FERPA）障碍，而现有联邦学习方案对小型机构协作的适配性不足，亟需一种技术可行且隐私合规的跨机构机器学习方案。

Method: 基于PySyft构建半气隙架构的远程数据科学框架（高侧/低侧服务器），研究人员无法直接访问原始数据；评估三种合成数据生成方法，并提出Data-Type-Aware Templates方法以优先保障隐私。

Result: 三所机构间的分类性能保持一致（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695），严格符合FERPA合规要求，验证了RDS框架的技术可行性。

Conclusion: RDS-based PPML在教育场景中技术可行，为小型跨机构协作提供了联邦学习的实用替代方案。

Abstract: This study explores privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML) techniques using the PySyft platform to enable collaborative prediction of student retention between institutions. We developed a remote data science (RDS) framework with a semi-air-gapped architecture consisting of high-side and low-side servers, allowing researchers from three universities to build predictive models on sensitive student data without direct data access. Using historical data from a small private university (N=720), we evaluated three synthetic data generation approaches and validated the framework through inter-institutional collaboration. The results demonstrate consistent classification performance across institutions (Macro F1: 0.690--0.695) while maintaining strict Family Educational Rights and Privacy Act (FERPA) compliance. We also propose Data-Type-Aware Templates, a novel synthetic data method that prioritizes privacy over distributional fidelity. Our findings confirm that RDS-based PPML is technically feasible for educational settings and offers a practical alternative to federated learning for small-scale inter-institutional collaborations. The code is available at https://github.com/jtfields/NAIRR240195-Privacy-Preserving-Machine-Learning.

</details>


### [77] [LNTest: A Testbed for Evaluating Bitcoin Lightning Network-Based Botnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12887)
*Thomas Bakaysa,Ahmet Kurt,Abdul-Salem Beibitkhan,Jesus Maria Romo Diaz de Leon,Tag Kalat,Joshua Kramer,Estela Rodriguez,Abraham Watkins,Abdullah Aydeger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LNTest——首个针对基于闪电网络(LN)的僵尸网络的可重用测试平台，发现D-LNBot的自主形成协议无法生成设计的均匀链而是产生聚集链，命令传播复杂度为Θ(n)而非此前声称的O(m log n)，且覆盖拓扑结构决定了下架策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LNBot和D-LNBot设计仅通过仿真作为概念验证原型进行评估，未回答真实世界的拓扑形成、传播复杂度和抗下架能力等关键问题。

Method: 基于Core Lightning节点和Docker容器化技术，构建在共享Bitcoin Core regtest链上的LNTest测试平台，支持三种覆盖拓扑模式（确定性链、自主对等发现、用户提供图），进行受控实验。

Result: (1) D-LNBot自主形成协议产生聚集链而非均匀链，桥节点移除即可分割网络；(2) 命令传播线性缩放(Θ(n))，而非此前声称的O(m log n)；(3) 均匀度链抵抗定向移除但易受随机故障影响，无尺度拓扑相反，自主聚集链在两种攻击下均脆弱。

Conclusion: LNTest已开源并提供可复现实验脚本，支持基于LN的僵尸网络防御的可重复研究。

Abstract: Bitcoin's Lightning Network (LN) can be exploited as a covert, low-cost command-and-control (C&C) channel for botnets, as demonstrated by the LNBot and D-LNBot designs. However, both remain proof-of-concept prototypes evaluated only through simulation, leaving key questions about real-world topology formation, propagation complexity, and resilience to takedowns unanswered. We present LNTest, the first reusable testbed for LN-based botnets, built from Core Lightning nodes containerized with Docker over a shared Bitcoin Core regtest chain. LNTest supports three overlay topology modes (a deterministic chain, autonomous peer discovery, and user-supplied graphs), enabling controlled experiments across different botnet structures. Using LNTest, we report three main findings. First, D-LNBot's autonomous formation protocol does not produce the uniform chain from its design; instead, it creates a clustered chain in which cliques are linked by bridge nodes whose removal fragments the network. Second, command propagation scales linearly with botnet size ($Θ(n)$), not the $O(m \log n)$ previously claimed, and gains nothing from higher neighbor connectivity. Third, the overlay topology determines the effectiveness of takedown strategies: uniform-degree chains resist targeted removal but fragment under random failure, scale-free topologies show the opposite pattern, and the autonomous clustered chain is fragile under both, making it the most vulnerable of the three. LNTest is released as open source, with a script that reproduces all our experiments, to support reproducible research on LN-based botnet defenses.

</details>


### [78] [MAStrike: Shapley-Guided Collusive Red-Teaming on Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12918)
*Chejian Xu,Zhaorun Chen,Jingyang Zhang,Freddy Lecue,Avni Kothari,Sarah Tan,Wenbo Guo,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MAStrike提出了一种面向分层多智能体系统的闭环红队测试框架，通过智能体级Shapley值分析识别脆弱智能体联盟，并生成协调的角色感知对抗攻击，显著优于启发式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS红队测试方法局限于启发式选择目标智能体和扰动孤立消息流，无法回答哪些智能体对系统安全最负责，以及被攻陷的智能体如何协同绕过防御的问题。

Method: 提出首个面向MAS的智能体级Shapley值分析方法，量化每个智能体对系统鲁棒性的边际贡献；基于此识别脆弱智能体联盟并生成协调的角色感知对抗攻击；通过结构化因果诊断迭代优化攻击。

Result: 在金融、软件工程和CRM等多个领域的多样化分层拓扑环境上，MAStrike大幅优于启发式基线方法，并揭示了非平凡的Shapley值分布和智能体间高阶交互结构。

Conclusion: MAStrike揭示了被先前方法忽略的关键脆弱性和协调模式，为分层MAS的安全性分析提供了更系统化的工具。

Abstract: Hierarchical multi-agent systems (MAS) are rapidly being deployed in high-stakes workflows across domains such as finance and software engineering. In these systems, safety and security are inherently distributed across role-specialized agents, significantly expanding the attack surface, particularly under coordinated adversarial behaviors such as privilege escalation and cross-agent collusion. Existing red-teaming approaches for MAS remain limited: they rely on heuristic selection of target agents and perturb isolated message streams, leaving critical questions unanswered as which agents are most responsible for system safety, and how compromised agents can coordinate to bypass defenses. We propose MAStrike, a closed-loop framework for collusive red-teaming in hierarchical MAS. We propose the first agent-level Shapley value analysis for MAS, quantifying each agent's marginal contribution to system robustness under task-specific distributions. GGuided by this attribution, MAStrike identifies vulnerable agent coalitions and generates coordinated, role-aware adversarial manipulations. These attacks are iteratively refined through structured causal diagnosis, attributing failure cases to uncompromised agents that block adversarial attempts. We further build a comprehensive MAS red-teaming benchmark and controllable environments spanning diverse hierarchical topologies and domains, including finance, software engineering, and CRM. Extensive experiments across MAS built on multiple frontier models show that MAStrike substantially outperforms heuristic baselines. Our analysis further uncovers non-trivial Shapley value distributions and higher-order interaction structures among agents, revealing critical vulnerabilities and coordination patterns that are overlooked by prior single-agent or template-based methods.

</details>


### [79] [ViPER: Vision-based Packing-Aware Encoder for Robust Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12949)
*Fatima Qaiser,Bisma Tahir,Muhammad Abid Mughal,Nauman Shamim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ViPER提出了一种基于视觉的打包感知编码器，通过LoRA适配的ViT-B/14双头架构，联合学习恶意软件分类和打包检测，利用打包感知门控机制和频率加权损失，在20万Windows PE样本上达到了0.8521的平衡准确率和0.9260的ROC-AUC，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于可视化技术的恶意软件检测方法在面对可执行文件打包时存在严重失效问题——打包后的二进制文件产生高熵图像，掩盖了模型依赖的结构模式。由于打包也广泛存在于良性软件中，仅靠打包状态无法可靠判别恶意性，而现有方法未能在统一监督框架下解决这一挑战。

Method: ViPER基于LoRA适配的ViT-B/14骨干网络，采用双头架构联合学习恶意软件分类和打包检测；通过打包感知门控机制使恶意软件预测条件化于推断的打包状态，为打包和未打包输入建立不同的决策边界；采用频率加权损失结合联合类别-打包分层的分层采样策略处理训练中的打包标签偏斜问题。

Result: 在20万Windows PE字节图图像上，ViPER达到平衡准确率0.8521、ROC-AUC 0.9260、AUPR 0.9279，在所有主要指标上均优于代表性基线方法，同时打包检测AUC达到0.9949。

Conclusion: ViPER通过将打包检测与恶意软件分类联合学习，有效解决了可视化检测中打包导致的性能退化问题，为鲁棒的恶意软件检测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Visualization-based malware detection maps raw binary bytes to grayscale images and applies learned visual classifiers, providing an evasion-resistant and disassembly-free alternative to conventional analysis pipelines. However, executable packing remains a critical failure mode: packed binaries produce high-entropy images that obscure the structural patterns these models rely on. Because packing is also prevalent in benign software (e.g., for compression or copy protection), packing state alone is not a reliable indicator of maliciousness, and existing approaches do not address this challenge within a unified supervised framework. We present ViPER, a Vision-based Packing-Aware Encoder for Robust malware detection. ViPER builds on a LoRA-adapted ViT-B/14 backbone with a dual-head architecture that jointly learns malware classification and packing detection. A packing-aware gating mechanism conditions malware predictions on the inferred packing state, enabling distinct decision boundaries for packed and unpacked inputs. To address packing label skew during training, we employ frequency-weighted losses with stratified sampling over joint class-packing strata. Evaluated on 200,000 Windows PE byteplot images, ViPER achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.8521, ROC-AUC of 0.9260, and AUPR of 0.9279, outperforming representative state-of-the-art baselines across all primary metrics, while attaining a packing detection AUC of 0.9949.

</details>


### [80] [SoK: The Constant Time Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13000)
*Billy Bob Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化了常数时间编程模型及其演变，发现了模型保护范围与规范假设之间的鸿沟，并提出了一种发现密码学原语边界之外时序漏洞的攻击方法论。通过该方法，定位到私钥加载相关的规范级漏洞，并在OpenSSL和BoringSSL中确认了信息泄漏。值得注意的是，尽管BoringSSL威胁模型更严格，但其单次观测信号强度比OpenSSL高出数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有常数时间实现作为防御时序攻击的主要手段，但学术界和工业界对"常数时间"的定义不一致，存在模型保护范围与密码学规范假设之间的系统性差距，导致时序漏洞可能出现在密码学原语边界之外。

Method: 系统化梳理常数时间模型的演变，识别模型与规范之间的鸿沟，提炼出发现密码学原语边界之外时序漏洞的攻击方法论。

Result: 发现私钥加载相关的规范级漏洞，在OpenSSL和BoringSSL中确认泄漏；BoringSSL单次观测信号强度比OpenSSL高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: 常数时间模型与实际密码学规范之间存在盲区，即使威胁模型更严格的实现也可能出现更强的侧信道泄漏，需要将防护边界扩展至密码学原语之外。

Abstract: Constant time programming patterns is the primary defense against timing attacks on cryptographic implementations, yet what "constant time" means varies across academia and industry. This work systematizes constant time models and their evolution, identifies a recurring gap between what models protect and what specifications assume, and distills an offensive methodology for discovering timing vulnerabilities that originate outside the cryptographic primitive boundary. Applying this methodology, we locate a specification-level vulnerability related to private key loading, and confirm the leak in both OpenSSL and BoringSSL. Counterintuitively, BoringSSL's per-observation signal is several orders of magnitude stronger than OpenSSL's, despite an explicitly stricter threat model.

</details>


### [81] [DIG: Oracle-Guided Directed Input Generation for One-Day Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13037)
*Andrew Bao,Haochen Zeng,Peng Chen,Stephen McCamant,Pen-Chung Yew*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DIG提出了一种利用补丁分析生成oracle（约束条件）来指导PoC生成的方法，结合LLM驱动的约束求解和定向模糊测试，在138个真实CVE上超越2个SOTA智能体和10个模糊测试器，触发80个漏洞（领先基线40%），独家触发9个现有技术无法触发的漏洞，最快加速3664倍，并发现6个零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单日漏洞因补丁延迟或未完全部署而构成重大风险，生成PoC输入对评估实际影响至关重要。现有定向模糊测试依赖目标距离反馈和随机变异，未明确识别必要约束；智能体方法在长程推理中存在目标漂移问题。需要一种能显式提取并有效求解触发漏洞必要约束的方法。

Method: DIG利用单日漏洞的一个关键属性：补丁往往揭示了触发漏洞的必要前置条件。使用LLM分析补丁并合成oracle显式化这些条件。在高层次，执行oracle引导的生成器进化，智能体推断并求解约束以满足oracle；在低层次，将oracle注入目标程序，利用分支距离反馈指导定向模糊测试中的随机变异。

Result: 在138个真实CVE上，DIG触发80个漏洞，超越2个SOTA智能体和10个模糊测试器，比最优基线高出40%（57 vs. 80）。独家触发9个现有技术无法触发的漏洞。相比其他工具平均值，92.9%的情况下更快触发漏洞，48.8%的情况下加速超100倍，最大加速3664倍。额外发现6个零日漏洞。

Conclusion: DIG通过补丁驱动的oracle合成与分层求解机制，有效解决了单日漏洞PoC生成中的约束识别与求解难题，显著提升了触发效率和覆盖范围，并具备零日漏洞发现能力。

Abstract: One-day vulnerabilities pose significant risks due to delayed or incomplete patch adoption. Generating proof-of-concept (PoC) inputs is therefore essential for assessing real-world impact. The key challenge is identifying necessary constraints for triggering the vulnerability and solving them effectively. Existing directed fuzzing approaches prioritize inputs toward target locations, but neither explicitly identify necessary constraints nor solve them effectively, relying instead on target-distance feedback and random mutation. Agentic approaches show strong potential through code reasoning and structured input generation, but goal drift in long-horizon reasoning limits their effectiveness.
  DIG addresses this challenge by exploiting a key property of one-day vulnerabilities: patches often reveal necessary preconditions for triggering. DIG uses an LLM to analyze the patch and synthesize an oracle making these conditions explicit. The oracle supports effective PoC generation at two levels. At the high level, DIG performs oracle-guided generator evolution, where an agent infers and solves constraints to satisfy the oracle. At the low level, DIG instruments the oracle into the target program and uses branch-distance feedback to guide random mutation in directed fuzzing. Evaluation shows DIG outperforms 2 state-of-the-art agents and 10 fuzzers across 138 real-world CVEs. DIG triggers 80 vulnerabilities, surpassing prior results and outperforming the best baseline by 40% (57 vs. 80 CVEs). Notably, DIG exclusively triggers 9 vulnerabilities no existing technique can trigger. Compared to the average of other tools, DIG triggers vulnerabilities faster in 92.9% of cases, achieving over 100x speedup in 48.8% of cases, with a maximum speedup of 3,664x. Beyond one-day PoC generation, DIG uncovers 6 previously unknown vulnerabilities in widely deployed libraries, enabling zero-day discovery.

</details>


### [82] [The Emergence of Autonomous Penetration Capabilities in Large Language Model-Powered AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13079)
*Jiaqi Luo,Jiarun Dai,Zhile Chen,Jia Xu,Weibing Wang,Yawen Duan,Brian Tse,Geng Hong,Xudong Pan,Yuan Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含两级目标环境（Tier-1和Tier-2，共300台目标服务器）和通用智能体框架的自主渗透评估体系，在无目标先验知识条件下评估了19个开源和商业LLM，发现当前模型的渗透成功率在10.7%到69.3%之间，且该能力随模型整体能力提升而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自主渗透能力评估存在方法论不透明、测试场景过于简化或脱离现实、以及向LLM提供过多先验知识和任务指导等问题，无法准确衡量现代AI系统在真实高影响网络攻击场景中的自主渗透能力。

Method: 构建包含两级目标环境（Tier-1含1个安全服务，Tier-2含3个安全服务，共300台服务器）和通用智能体架构（配备通用网络安全工具，无目标先验知识）的评估框架，对19个开源和商业LLM进行自主渗透能力测试。

Result: 当前模型自主渗透成功率范围为10.7%到69.3%，且自主渗透能力随模型整体能力提升而持续改进。

Conclusion: 该评估框架能更准确地衡量LLM的自主渗透能力，揭示了当前前沿模型在该能力上已有显著进展但仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Nowadays, the autonomous execution of cyberattacks capable of causing substantial real-world harm is widely regarded as one of the critical red lines that frontier AI systems must not cross. Within this broader red-line scenario, autonomous penetration represents a core enabling capability and subtask: the ability of LLM-powered AI systems to independently conduct adversarial operations against a target server without human intervention, identify and exploit vulnerabilities, and obtain unauthorized access or control. A growing body of work has sought to assess the autonomous penetration capabilities of AI systems. However, existing evaluations often employ opaque methodologies, rely on unrealistic or overly simplified penetration-testing scenarios, or provide LLMs with excessive prior knowledge and task-specific guidance, and cannot accurately capture the extent to which modern AI systems can autonomously perform this core capability within broader high-impact cyberattack scenarios.
  To address these limitations, we construct a new autonomous penetration evaluation framework consisting of two components: target servers and agent scaffolding. Specifically, on the target-server side, we design two levels of target environments based on the number of secure services without known vulnerabilities deployed alongside a vulnerable service: Tier~1 (one secure service) and Tier~2 (three secure services), resulting in a total of 300 target servers. Meanwhile, the agent scaffolding adopts a general-purpose agent architecture equipped with a set of general-purpose cybersecurity tools, without any target-specific prior knowledge. We evaluate 19 open-weight and proprietary LLMs, and find that current models achieve penetration success rates ranging from 10.7% to 69.3%. Moreover, we observe that autonomous penetration capability continues to improve alongside advances in overall model capability.

</details>


### [83] [The Invisible Ink of the Android Malware World: A Longitudinal Study on the Usage of Covert Communication Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13107)
*Zeya Umayya,Manan Aggarwal,Manan Chugh,Mann Nariya,Yogesh Kaushik,Sambuddho Chakravarty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对2009年至2025年间350万Android恶意软件进行首次纵向研究，发现使用隐蔽信道(CC)的APK达28.8万，涵盖511个恶意软件家族，CC使用率从2012年的0.30%激增至2025年的50%。动态分析识别出85个国家的19,308个IP地址，其中59个IP（17个国家）被验证存在CC。部分恶意软件家族在2019-2025年间切换CC达40次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代理、VPN和Tor等隐私工具被恶意软件滥用于隐蔽通信，且恶意软件攻击日益泛滥，但尚无纵向研究分析恶意应用如何使用隐蔽信道(CC)来规避检测，存在研究空白。

Method: 开发了结合静态与动态分析的多阶段管道，对350万Android恶意软件（2009-2025年）进行系统和网络层面特征分析，包含精心设计的静态验证规则。

Result: 静态分析发现288K APK使用CC（511个家族），CC使用率从0.30%（2012）增长到50%（2025）。动态分析识别19,308个IP（85国），其中59个IP（17国）验证存在CC。发现CC使用演变，如部分恶意软件使用多种CC，一个家族在2019-2025年间切换CC达40次。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了Android恶意软件生态中隐蔽信道使用纵向研究的空白，揭示了CC使用呈指数级增长和演变的趋势，对恶意软件检测和防御具有重要启示。

Abstract: Proxies, VPNs and Tor have long helped the privacy community and users in censored regions to fight censorship. However, the same tools can be maliciously exploited by malware and botnets to conceal their communication to external command and control servers. Despite being a critical concern fueled by the proliferation of malware based attacks, no longitudinal studies have analyzed how malware applications use covert channels (CC) to evade detection.
  We fill this gap by performing the first study of the usage of covert channels in the Android malware ecosystem. To that end, we develop a multistage pipeline that combines static and dynamic analysis to investigate both system and network-level features. We applied this pipeline on a corpus of 3.5M Android malware spanning 2009 to July 2025. Our carefully crafted static validation rules uncovered 288K APKs that used CCs spanning 511 malware families and CC usage growing exponentially from 0.30\% (2012) to 50\% (2025). Overall, in dynamic analysis, we identified 19,308 unique IP addresses being contacted in 85 countries, out of which we were able to explicitly validate the presence of CCs for 59 IP addresses across 17 countries. Further, we performed a longitudinal dataset study spanning over 16 years for CC based malware and found that CC usage has evolved, \textit{e.g.,} some malware adopted by using more than one CCs; others switched between them periodically (one family switched CC usage 40 times from 2019 to 2025).

</details>


### [84] [Who Pays the Price? Stakeholder-Centric Prompt Injection Benchmarking for Real-world Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13385)
*Zihao Wang,Yiming Li,Yutong Wu,Zheyu Liu,Kangjie Chen,Fok Kar Wai,Pin-Yu Chen,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing,Bo Li,Dacheng Tao,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 StakeBench，一个以利益相关者为中心的基准测试，用于系统性地分类和归因 LLM 驱动的 Web 代理在现实世界中面临的提示注入攻击风险，揭示了当前代理无法可靠抵抗任何攻击目标，并识别出多种被传统评估忽视的失败模式（如隐蔽寄生、错位干扰和复合失败）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准采用以攻击为中心的视角，仅关注注入的技术可行性，忽视了攻击对不同利益相关者（用户、卖家、平台等）造成的非对称后果和差异化影响，无法捕捉真实世界中提示注入风险的受害者依赖性特征。

Method: 构建利益相关者中心基准，区分受影响实体（用户、卖家、平台），将攻击分解为具体目标，并采用结果级和过程级互补指标进行评估。

Result: 当前代理无法可靠抵抗任何单一攻击目标；失败模式包含隐蔽寄生（攻击成功且不干扰用户任务）、错位干扰（任务中断但攻击未成功）和复合失败（攻击目标与任务完整性同时被破坏）等异质模式。

Conclusion: 传统评估方法无法发现上述模式，需要采用利益相关者感知的评估方法来评估现实部署中基于 LLM 的代理。

Abstract: Web agents driven by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world environments, where they operate over untrusted web content and execute actions with direct consequences. This makes them vulnerable to prompt-injection attacks, in which seemingly benign content embeds adversarial instructions that manipulate agent behaviour. Existing security benchmarks adopt an \textit{attack-centric} perspective, focusing on the technical feasibility of injections while overlooking the nuanced distribution of resulting harms. In practice, however, prompt-injection risk is victim-dependent: a single exploit can produce asymmetric consequences for different stakeholders, and the same attack pattern may exhibit substantially different effectiveness depending on whom it targets. To capture these properties, we introduce \textbf{\sysname}, a \textit{stakeholder-centric} benchmark to systematically categorize and attribute harm in real-world web agent systems. It distinguishes between affected entities (e.g., user, seller, platform), decomposes the attacks into concrete objectives, and evaluates each case with complementary outcome- and process-level metrics. Our results reveal substantial and heterogeneous vulnerabilities: not a single attack objective is reliably resisted by current agents, and failures distribute across qualitatively distinct modes ranging from \emph{stealthy parasitism} (attack succeeds without disrupting the user's delegated task) to \emph{misaligned disruption} (task disrupted without attack success) and \emph{compounded failure} (both adversarial objective and task integrity simultaneously violated). These patterns are missed by conventional evaluation, highlighting the need for stakeholder-aware assessment of LLM-based agents in real-world deployments. Benchmark is available at https://github.com/StakeBench/SBC.

</details>


### [85] [An Assessment Framework for Application-Level Cryptographic Agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13425)
*Navaneeth Rameshan,Gregoire Messmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个七维组件化评估框架，用于系统性地衡量应用层面的密码敏捷性。通过对六个代表性API的评估，发现三个普遍存在的关键缺口：缺乏意图驱动的密钥创建、缺乏策略驱动的算法选择、缺乏密钥算法转换操作，这些缺口解释了为何后量子迁移至今仍是软件工程难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前密码学API在设计时未将敏捷性作为考量，导致后量子迁移面临巨大挑战。缺乏系统性的方法来评估密码敏捷性，且该术语本身涵盖多种独立能力（算法替换、策略选择、实现替换），这种缺乏结构化分解的现状限制了对系统的评估和敏捷API的开发。

Method: 提出一个组件化评估框架，从七个正交维度刻画应用层面密码敏捷性：三个耦合维度（衡量应用代码对算法和提供商的了解程度）、一个横切解耦机制、一个治理权威维度、两个敏捷性使能维度（衡量实际迁移能力）。框架是非线性的，能够捕捉非层次化的敏捷性剖面。使用该框架评估PKCS#11、OpenSSL 3.0、JCA、Google Tink、AWS KMS和HashiCorp Vault Transit六个代表性API。

Result: 六个API存在三个普遍且独立的能力缺口：1) 无系统支持意图驱动的密钥创建；2) 无系统提供策略驱动的算法选择（区别于访问控制）；3) 无系统提供专有的密钥算法转换操作。这些缺口各自都足以阻碍敏捷迁移。

Conclusion: 尽管密码学API经过数十年发展，上述三个能力缺口各自独立且足以阻碍敏捷迁移，这解释了为何后量子迁移至今仍是一个软件工程问题。该框架为系统评估和改进密码敏捷性提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: The impending post-quantum transition to new cryptography will require complete replacement of algorithms within all software. The cryptographic APIs used today make this transition challenging because they were not designed with agility as a concern. There is no method for systematically assessing cryptographic agility as an overall ability. In addition to this, the term itself refers to multiple independent capabilities. Specifically, it includes replacing algorithms, selecting by policy, and substituting implementations. This lack of structured decomposition limits both the evaluation of systems and the development of cryptographically agile APIs.
  We introduce a component-based assessment framework that characterizes application-level cryptographic agility along seven orthogonal dimensions: three coupling dimensions that measure what the application code knows about algorithms and providers, a cross-cutting decoupling mechanism, a governance authority dimension, and two agility enablers that measure actual migration capability. The framework is non-linear and captures non-hierarchical profiles: a system may achieve high operation decoupling yet low creation decoupling, or strong versioning without externalized configuration.
  We evaluate six representative APIs (PKCS#11, OpenSSL~3.0, JCA, Google Tink, AWS KMS, and HashiCorp Vault Transit) against the framework, revealing three pervasive and independent gaps: no system supports intent-based key creation, none provides policy-driven algorithm selection (as distinct from access control), and none offers dedicated/first-class operations for algorithm transformation of existing keys. These gaps are individually sufficient to prevent agile migration, explaining why the post-quantum transition remains a software engineering problem despite decades of API progress.

</details>


### [86] [Intent-Based Cryptographic API Design for Cryptographic Agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13445)
*Navaneeth Rameshan,Gregoire Messmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出当前密码学API因紧耦合特定算法而难以支持向抗量子密码迁移，提出了基于抽象、稳定、时间灵活、分离和可扩展五项架构原则的密码敏捷API设计方法，并通过Protocol Buffers设计模式实现了意图词汇表、密钥演化与密码治理策略，使得密码更新成为无需修改应用代码的运维过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在向后量子密码迁移时面临密码算法更新困难，现有密码学API紧耦合特定算法、缺乏策略支持、无法迁移现有密钥，导致算法过渡成为严重的软件工程问题。

Method: 从五项基本架构特征（抽象、稳定、时间灵活、分离、可扩展）推导出设计原则，利用Protocol Buffers API设计模式实现基于作用域的意图词汇表来解耦密钥创建与算法身份，并支持算法透明替换；通过抽象策略API实现密码治理，用稳定标识符表示密钥并支持轮换、变换、迁移等演化操作。

Result: 提出了一套密码敏捷API设计方案，支持密钥与算法解耦、算法透明替换、密钥演化操作以及策略驱动的密码治理，使得密码更新成为运维操作而无需重写应用代码。

Conclusion: 通过遵循所提出的设计原则和实现模式，可以构建密码学敏捷的API，有效降低向后量子密码迁移的工程难度，将密码更新从代码修改转变为可管理的运维流程。

Abstract: As organizations move toward post-quantum cryptography, they face the major challenge of updating cryptographic algorithms across large, complex software portfolios. However, most cryptographic APIs in use today were designed around specific algorithms. These APIs expect explicit use of specific algorithms, provide little or no support for policy-based algorithm selection, and offer no straightforward way to migrate existing keys to newer algorithms. This makes the transition to post-quantum cryptography challenging. The companion assessment framework identifies the barriers to cryptographic agility and explains why algorithm transition is largely a software engineering problem.
  To address the limitations of current cryptographic APIs, we identify the principles necessary to design a cryptographically agile API. The design principles are derived from five fundamental architectural characteristics (Abstraction, Stability, Temporal Flexibility, Separation, and Extensibility). We also show how the design principles can be implemented using several examples of Protocol Buffers API design patterns. In particular, we present an intent vocabulary that is based on scopes which allows for decoupling key creation from algorithm identities. It also supports transparent substitutions of algorithms in the applicable scope. Cryptographic governance is enabled by an abstract policy API that does not prescribe the policy format. Keys are represented by stable identifiers and support key evolution operations (rotation, transformation, migration), facilitating migration between algorithms and providers while tracking both the original key identity and its evolution history. With this approach, updating cryptography becomes an operational process without the need to rewrite application code.

</details>


### [87] [Differentially Private Hierarchical Heavy Hitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13563)
*Ari Biswas,Graham Cormode,Yaron Kanza,Divesh Srivastava,Zhengyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了差分隐私下的层次化重击者（HHH）发布问题，涵盖非流式和流式两种场景。非流式下，残差计数估计的相对误差与层次高度和重击者数量无关；流式下，虽然近似函数具有高全局敏感度，但频率估计的绝对误差与可用空间无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HHH问题自Cormode等人提出以来得到了广泛研究，但数据隐私情境下的HHH发布问题尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究差分隐私保护下的HHH发布。

Method: 分别在非流式和流式两种设置下分析HHH的差分隐私发布问题。在非流式设置中，分析前缀残差计数估计的相对误差；在流式设置中，分析由于流式处理引入的近似函数对全局敏感度的影响。

Result: 非流式设置下，前缀残差计数的相对误差与层次高度和重击者数量无关。流式设置下，尽管近似函数具有与可用空间成线性关系的高全局敏感度，但频率估计的绝对误差与可用空间无关。

Conclusion: 本文首次将差分隐私引入HHH问题，并在两种设置下得到了令人惊喜的误差界结果，为隐私保护的流式数据分析提供了新的理论支撑。

Abstract: The task of finding _Hierarchical_ Heavy Hitters (HHH) was introduced by Cormode et al. [VLDB 2003] as a generalisation of the heavy hitter problem. While finding HHH in data streams has been studied extensively, the question of releasing HHH when the underlying data is private remains unexplored. In this paper, we study differentially private HHH release in both the streaming and non-streaming setting. In the non-streaming setting, we show the surprising result that the relative error in estimating the residual count for any prefix is independent of the height of the hierarchy and the number of heavy hitters in the stream. Meanwhile, in the streaming setting, although the exact version of HHH has low global sensitivity (as counting queries are 1-sensitive), the approximation functions due to streaming have high global sensitivity, linear in the available space. Despite this obstacle, we show that the absolute error for estimating frequencies in the steaming setting is independent of the available space.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond the IT Checklist: Engineering a Reasonable Standard of Care for Cyber Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13612)
*Matthew E. Jablonski,Linton Wells,Kathryn B. Laskey,F. Brett Berlin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了美国网络政策（2000-2025年，N=292份文件）在关键基础设施中如何操作化"合理注意"标准，发现义务集中在"预测"阶段且偏重行政合规，而"承受"和"恢复"阶段过度依赖与物理风险脱节的IT控制目录。作者提出基于危害可追溯性、结构化保证案例和网络弹性工程的现代化注意标准，并建议联邦政策配套激励措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现行美国网络政策以安全为中心，将控制文档和事件报告作为安全代理，但这种方法对可能产生动能危害的网络物理系统不充分，需要重新审视"合理注意"标准的适用性。

Method: 构建并编码了2000-2025年间292份关键基础设施政策文档的语料库，沿NIST SP 800-160 Vol.2弹性生命周期分析"合理注意"的操作化方式。

Result: 义务集中于"预测"阶段且强调行政合规；"承受"和"恢复"阶段过度依赖指向IT控制目录的委托引用，与物理风险脱节。识别出三大脱节：校准不当的委托标准、恢复被定义为通知而非工程化导航、跨部门适应性要求不均。

Conclusion: 提出基于危害可追溯性、结构化保证案例和网络弹性工程的现代化注意标准，并建议联邦政策配套激励措施使弹性架构成为可行的商业决策。

Abstract: Current U.S. cyber policy, centered on security, often treats documentation of controls and incident reports as a proxy for safety in the built environment. This paper argues that such an approach is inadequate for cyber-physical systems, where digital failures can produce kinetic harm. We construct and code a corpus of critical infrastructure policy documents (N=292, 2000-2025) to examine how "reasonable care" is operationalized across the NIST SP 800-160 Vol.~2 resilience lifecycle. The resulting maps show that obligations are concentrated in the Anticipate phase and emphasize administrative compliance, while Withstand and Recover phases rely heavily on delegated references to IT-focused control catalogs that are poorly aligned with physics-based hazards. We identify three major disconnects: miscalibrated delegated standards, recovery defined as notification rather than engineered navigation, and uneven adaptation requirements across sectors. We then propose a modernized standard of care anchored in hazard-specific traceability, structured assurance cases, and cyber resiliency engineering. Finally, we recommend that federal policy pair these engineering obligations with targeted incentives so that resilient architectures for critical infrastructure become a viable business decision rather than an unfunded expectation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolSense诊断框架，用于评估LLM作为工具检索器时的真实理解能力。发现在标准基准上表现优异的参数化工具检索模型，在面对模糊查询时性能大幅下降（最高降64个百分点），且存在知识-检索分离现象：模型能检索到工具却不理解其语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索基准使用完整详细的查询语句，且评估采用约束解码限制输出为有效token路径，无法反映模型是否真正理解工具语义。需要更全面的诊断框架来检测模型对工具的真实理解程度。

Method: 提出ToolSense框架，能自动生成三类基准测试：1) 真实检索基准（RRB），包含三种模糊程度级别的查询；2) MCQ探测基准；3) QA探测基准。在ToolBench（~47k工具）上评估了五种参数化模型训练配置。

Result: 在RRB查询上，多个配置相比标准ToolBench基准性能下降50-64个百分点，低于嵌入模型基线。尽管检索性能强，部分模型在事实探测上接近随机水平，表明存在知识-检索分离。

Conclusion: 参数化工具检索模型存在知识-检索分离问题，在模糊查询场景下性能崩塌，且检索能力强不等于模型真正理解工具。ToolSense框架可用于诊断此类问题并推动改进。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [90] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor是一个多智能体框架，通过结构化树搜索作为认知层，实现大规模有状态动作空间中的自主优化。在LLM推理优化领域，通过Orchestrator和Critic双智能体制衡架构，配合硬技能与软技能分解，在无需人工干预的多日自动化优化中，实现了比供应商基线高达193%的推理吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主优化系统处理的是孤立目标和无状态评估，无法应对需要跨全栈协调的复杂优化任务（如LLM推理优化），这类任务历史上依赖跨应用、框架、编译器、内核和硬件栈的工程团队协同努力。

Method: 提出结构化树搜索作为自主智能体的认知层；引入Orchestrator（驱动优化）和Critic（通过根因分析、内省和测量验证保障稳定性）的双智能体制衡架构；将智能体能力分解为硬技能（领域专长）和软技能（协调协议）。

Result: 相对于供应商优化基线实现高达193%的推理吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进；无框架的单智能体吞吐量仅提升+33%且在数小时内不可恢复地崩溃；方法跨多代硬件平台泛化，运行间方差在2个百分点以内。

Conclusion: Arbor证明了结构化树搜索作为认知层、双智能体制衡架构以及技能分解策略，能够实现硬件无关、可复现的全自主多日优化，显著超越单智能体系统和供应商基线。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [91] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向AI智能体的战略决策支持框架，通过优化问题最小化支持使用量，同时控制反事实遗漏支持错误（即智能体在需要支持时独自行动的概率），并设计了在线自适应阈值算法，在信息收集、人机协作和工具使用等场景中有效控制目标错误并减少支持调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策支持研究关注人类如何利用机器学习模型做出更好决策，但在现代智能体系统中角色发生了逆转：AI智能体代表用户行动，人类和工具成为支持机制。这种角色反转带来了可靠性问题，因为智能体错误后果严重，且其行为必须与人类目标和约束保持一致。本文重新审视决策支持的两个基本原则（寻求支持的成本-价值权衡和不确定性量化的作用），以适应AI智能体作为核心行动者的新场景。

Method: 提出一个优化框架，通过最小化支持使用量，同时控制反事实遗漏支持错误（即智能体在那些支持本可实质性改善其输出的实例上独自行动的概率）。在总体层面证明最优策略是支持价值的阈值规则。基于此结构，开发了一种在线自适应阈值算法，利用随机探索来控制遗漏支持错误，无需分布假设。进一步引入在线校准方法以减少不必要的支持调用。

Result: 在信息收集、人机协作和工具使用等多种场景中进行了实验，结果表明该方法能够可靠地控制目标错误，同时在实际中大幅减少支持使用量。

Conclusion: 本文框架将传统决策支持的理念迁移到AI智能体系统，通过战略性地分配支持资源，在保证可靠性的前提下提高效率，为构建更可信的自主智能体系统提供了理论指导和实用算法。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions. In modern agentic systems, this division of roles is increasingly reversed: AI agents act on behalf of users, while humans and tools becomes support mechanisms around them. This role reversal brings reliability concerns to the forefront, since agentic errors can be consequential and agent behavior must remain aligned with human goals and constraints. Departing from the classical view of decision support, we revisit its two basic principles, the cost--value tradeoff of seeking support and the role of uncertainty quantification, in a setting where AI agents are the central actors. We propose a framework for strategic decision support for AI agents through an optimization problem that minimizes support usage subject to controlling a counterfactual missed-support error: the probability that the agent acts alone on instances where support would have materially improved its output. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the value of support. Building on this structure, we develop an online algorithm that adaptively thresholds such a score and uses randomized exploration to control missed-support error without distributional assumptions. We further introduce a calibration-on-the-fly method that reduces unnecessary support calls online. We instantiate this framework across diverse scenarios, including information gathering, human--AI collaboration, and tool use, showing how each can be modeled through the same strategic decision-support lens. Experiments across these settings show that our method reliably controls the target error while substantially reducing support usage in practice.

</details>


### [92] [Pythagoras-Prover: Advancing Efficient Formal Proving via Augmented Lean Formalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12594)
*Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Zheng Zhao,Mihaela Cătălina Stoian,Qiyuan Xu,Haonan Li,Wenda Li,Shay B. Cohen,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pythagoras-Prover，一个计算高效的Lean定理证明器家族，包含4B和32B的自回归模型以及首个基于扩散的证明器（4B）。通过课程SFT、动态证明推理过滤和增强形式化（ALF）数据扩展技术，Pythagoras-Prover-4B以167倍少的参数超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B（86.1% vs 82.4%），32B模型在MiniF2F-Test上达到93.0%并解决93道PutnamBench问题，均达到开源最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Lean定理证明器需要大量训练和推理计算资源，主要因为已验证的证明数据稀缺以及形式化证明搜索的长推理轨迹，导致监督微调和推理采样成本高昂。本文旨在构建一个在实用计算预算下高效运行的定理证明器。

Method: 1）课程SFT：将Lean验证语料按难度（简单/中等/困难）分层，使模型渐进学习证明技能。2）动态证明推理过滤：保留信息性证明轨迹，同时将每个样本控制在8k token上下文预算内。3）增强形式化（ALF）：通过对已知问题的扰动生成形式化陈述变体，并利用自蒸馏自举生成额外训练信号，无需验证每个变异实例。

Result: Pythagoras-Prover-4B在pass@32上达到86.1%（MiniF2F-Test），超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B的82.4%，参数减少约167倍。32B模型在MiniF2F-Test上达到93.0%，解决93道PutnamBench问题。ALF增强的MiniF2F-ALF基准上所有模型准确率均下降，但32B仍保持最强，4B媲美Goedel-Prover-V2-32B。

Conclusion: 实验表明，通过课程学习、动态过滤和ALF数据扩展，可以在计算资源有限的情况下构建高性能Lean定理证明器，大幅超越同等甚至更大规模模型。这为资源受限场景下的形式化推理提供了可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Modern Lean theorem provers achieve strong performance only with substantial training and inference compute, driven in part by scarce verified proof data and the long reasoning traces of formal proof search, making both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and sampling expensive. We introduce Pythagoras-Prover, a compute-efficient open-source family of Lean theorem provers built for practical compute budgets. The family spans two generation paradigms: autoregressive models at 4B and 32B parameters, and a first proof-of-concept diffusion-based prover (4B) that iteratively refines Lean proofs at inference time. For training efficiency, we build a Lean-verified corpus stratified into easy, medium, and hard problems for curriculum SFT, so models acquire proof skills progressively from shorter, simpler proofs to longer, harder ones. During SFT, a dynamic proof-reasoning filtering scheme preserves informative proof traces while keeping each instance within an 8k-token context budget. We also introduce Augmented Lean Formalisation (ALF), which expands scarce verified corpora into variants of formal statements, populated via self-distillation for extra training signal without formally verifying every mutated instance. By perturbing known problems while preserving their formal character, ALF reduces reliance on any statement's surface form. Empirically, Pythagoras-Prover-4B surpasses DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B at pass@32 on MiniF2F-Test (86.1% vs 82.4%) with ~167x fewer parameters, while Pythagoras-Prover-32B sets the open-source state of the art at 93.0% on MiniF2F-Test and solves 93 of 672 PutnamBench problems. We release MiniF2F-ALF, an ALF-mutated contamination-sensitive benchmark on which every evaluated model loses accuracy; here our 32B remains strongest and our 4B matches the prior state of the art, Goedel-Prover-V2-32B.

</details>


### [93] [PersonaDrive: Human-Style Retrieval-Augmented VLA Agents for Closed-Loop Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12616)
*Mahmoud Srewa,Praneetsai Iddamsetty,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonaDrive 是一种基于检索的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）驾驶管线，通过风格指令下的人类驾驶演示（激进、中立、保守）来条件化驾驶智能体，无需针对每种风格重新训练即可实现风格多样的非自车智能体，并在 Bench2Drive 上取得优于现有方法的驾驶分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环驾驶模拟器中非自车交通智能体的行为单一，风格变化要么依赖后验标签要么依赖 LLM 推断的奖励权重，缺乏从明确要求人类以某种风格驾驶的真实演示中学习的方法。

Method: 三阶段流程：(i) 基于图像-文本相似度的离线三元组挖掘；(ii) 训练轻量级检索头，融合冻结的视觉特征与小控制编码器；(iii) 微调单一 VLA 主干网络，在预测路点时将检索到的上下文点作为上下文行为演示。推理时通过切换检索数据库实现风格条件化。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 上，无风格条件下驾驶分数比 SimLingo 提升 4.6%，比 HiP-AD 提升 2.5%；风格条件下每种风格均达到最高驾驶分数，最弱风格比最强基线 DMW 高 5.4%；从保守到激进指令，平均速度和加速度分别提升 18% 和 25%。

Conclusion: PersonaDrive 通过检索风格指令下的人类驾驶演示作为上下文示例，实现了无需重新训练的风格条件化驾驶，在性能和多风格一致性上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving simulators typically populate their environments with non-ego traffic agents that behave largely the same way, produced either by rule-based traffic managers or by learned models trained toward a single behavioral mode. Recent work introduces style variation through post-hoc labels on observational data or LLM-inferred reward weights, but these signals act as proxies for what a style should reward rather than demonstrations of humans explicitly asked to drive in that style. We introduce PersonaDrive, a pipeline that conditions a vision-language-action (VLA) driving agent on retrieved demonstrations from a style-instructed human driving dataset, in which participants drive CARLA leaderboard routes under aggressive, neutral, and conservative instructions on a driver-in-the-loop rig. The pipeline has three stages: (i) offline triplet mining over per-style human driving data using a combined image-text similarity score; (ii) training a lightweight retrieval head that fuses frozen visual features with a small control encoder over per-style databases; and (iii) fine-tuning a single VLA backbone to treat retrieved context points as in-context behavioral demonstrations during waypoint prediction. At inference, the same backbone is conditioned on any style by swapping which per-style database the retrieval head queries, so selecting a style requires no per-style retraining while enabling human-style, style-diverse non-ego agents for closed-loop simulation. On Bench2Drive, PersonaDrive (no style) improves the driving score by 4.6% over SimLingo and 2.5% over HiP-AD, and under style conditioning attains the highest driving score in every style within a roughly 2% band (its weakest style surpassing the strongest baseline, DMW, by 5.4%), while average speed and acceleration rise by 18% and 25% from the conservative to the aggressive instruction.

</details>


### [94] ["Did you lie?" Evaluating Lie Detectors across Model Scale and Belief-Verified Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12618)
*Alan Cooney,David Africa,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现现有谎言检测器在训练过的模型有机体上性能大幅下降，仅链式思维法官保持较强表现（平衡准确率0.82），表明当前谎言检测器无法高置信度地判断模型信念，并发布了相关数据集和检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练过的模型有机体往往不能满足模型真实信念与输出相反的测试要求，导致先前正面和负面的检测结果难以解释，因此需要构建可靠的测试平台来评估谎言检测器。

Method: 构建了13个推理模型有机体（通过链式思维验证隐藏信念并泛化到保留任务）和Varied Deception提示撒谎测试平台；评估了四种检测器：链式思维法官、对数概率分类器、两种激活探针，包括新提出的Did-You-Lie (DYL)后续探针训练方法。

Result: 在提示撒谎任务上，31个开源模型（2B到1T参数）的所有四种检测器均随模型能力正向缩放；但在训练过的模型有机体上，激活探针和对数概率检测器性能大幅下降，DYL保留最多信号；仅链式思维法官表现强劲（0.82平衡准确率），但部分归因于验证过程偏好可读链式思维信念。

Conclusion: 当前谎言检测器无法高置信度地判断模型信念，需进一步研究以解决现有局限性。

Abstract: Robust lie detectors for language models could enable powerful techniques for auditing, monitoring, and post-hoc investigation of model behaviour, but evaluating them requires testbeds where models verifiably believe the opposite of what they say. We show that existing trained model organisms often fail this requirement, leaving prior positive and negative detection results difficult to interpret. We address this with 13 reasoning model organisms whose hidden beliefs are verified in chain-of-thought and shown to generalise to held-out tasks, alongside Varied Deception, a prompted-lying testbed covering a broad range of lie-inducing motivations. On these testbeds we evaluate four detectors: a chain-of-thought judge, a logprob classifier, and two activation probes, including Did-You-Lie (DYL), a new method for training follow-up probes. On prompted lying, across 31 open-weight models spanning 2B to 1T parameters, all four detectors show positive scaling with model capability. However, every activation- and logprob-based detector drops sharply on our trained model organisms, with DYL retaining the most signal; only the chain-of-thought judge remains strong, with 0.82 balanced accuracy, partly as an artefact of our verification process favouring CoT-readable beliefs. Current lie detectors therefore cannot support high-confidence claims about model beliefs, and we suggest research directions that may address some of their current limitations. We release our datasets, model organisms, and trained detectors.

</details>


### [95] [TrajGenAgent: A Hierarchical LLM Agent for Human Mobility Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12657)
*Siyu Li,Toan Tran,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrajGenAgent 提出了一种无需微调的分层LLM智能体框架，用于生成真实的人类移动轨迹。它采用两阶段设计（活动链合成+确定性位置选择），并引入基于异常检测的评估方法，在保真度和语义合理性上优于现有的神经网络和LLM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模轨迹数据收集成本高且受隐私约束，需要真实合成轨迹生成方法。现有LLM方法要么依赖提示工程（缺乏细粒度时空基础），要么进行轨迹级微调（计算成本高、可能削弱通用推理能力），因此需要一种既能保持零样本推理能力又具备高时空精度的轨迹生成方法。

Method: 提出两阶段"组织者-工作者"架构：阶段一，LLM通过上下文学习基于历史证据合成个体和星期条件化的活动链；阶段二，确定性工作流通过个性化POI检索、距离感知位置选择、运动学感知时间传播和LLM持续时间估计将活动落地为完整访问。同时引入基于异常检测的评估框架，使用互补检测器评估行为与语义合理性。

Result: 在基准和大规模模拟数据集上，TrajGenAgent在时空保真度、语义一致性和个体特异性行为真实性方面均优于代表性神经网络和LLM基线方法，且无需参数更新。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的语义推理与确定性地理计算相结合，无需微调即可实现高质量轨迹生成，为隐私约束下的移动数据合成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Human mobility data is important for transportation, urban planning, and epidemic control, but large-scale trajectory collection is often costly and privacy-constrained, motivating realistic synthetic trajectory generation. Existing LLM-based generators typically rely on either prompt engineering, which preserves zero-shot reasoning but lacks fine-grained spatiotemporal grounding, or trajectory-level fine-tuning, which improves statistical precision but incurs substantial computational cost and may weaken general reasoning. We propose TrajGenAgent, a semantic-aware hierarchical LLM-agent framework for human mobility trajectory generation without model fine-tuning. TrajGenAgent uses a two-stage orchestrator-worker design: an LLM first synthesizes an individual- and weekday-conditioned activity chain from historical evidence via in-context learning, and a deterministic workflow then grounds each activity into a complete visit using personalized POI retrieval, distance-aware location selection, kinematics-aware travel-time propagation, and LLM-based duration estimation. To evaluate realism beyond aggregate spatiotemporal statistics, we introduce an anomaly-detection-based evaluation framework using two complementary detectors to assess behavioral and semantic plausibility. Experiments on benchmark and large-scale simulation datasets show that TrajGenAgent improves spatiotemporal fidelity, semantic coherence, and individual-specific behavioral realism over representative neural and LLM-based baselines, while avoiding parameter updates.

</details>


### [96] [Evoflux: Inference-Time Evolution of Executable Tool Workflows for Compact Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12674)
*Kushal Raj Bhandari,Ling Yue,Ching-Yun Ko,Dhaval Patel,Shaowu Pan,Pin-Yu Chen,Jianxi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Evoflux是一种推理时进化搜索方法，通过修复可执行工具工作流来提升小模型在MCP工具使用上的表现，在250个工具、MCP-Bench任务上将执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%，优于SFT、DPO和ReAct方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型在使用MCP风格工具时，容易生成看似合理但实际无法执行的工作流图，在工具解析、参数验证、依赖追踪和执行环节失败。传统的蒸馏方法（小语料蒸馏）难以覆盖修复失败计划的恢复行为，因此需要更可靠的执行导向搜索方法。

Method: 提出Evoflux，一种推理时进化搜索方法，核心思想是将小模型的工具使用问题转化为可执行工具工作流的修复问题。通过结构化编辑、执行反馈、自适应强度、元引导重新设计和多样性剪枝来进化类型化工作流图，从而搜索出可执行的工具调用序列。

Result: 在MCP-Bench任务上（覆盖实时MCP服务器和250个工具），Evoflux将小模型的执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%。SFT和SFT+DPO方法在相同搜索挖掘数据上表现不如零样本，或性能下降；ReAct能达到更高峰值但方差大、token成本高。

Conclusion: 在教师轨迹预算稀缺的情况下，基于执行反馈的搜索比蒸馏方法更可靠。执行导向的进化搜索是提升小模型工具使用能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Compact language models (LMs) reduce cost, latency, and deployment risk for tool agents. Yet MCP-style tool use requires more than isolated function calling: an agent must discover tools from live catalogs, satisfy schemas, preserve dependencies across intermediate outputs, and ground final responses in executed evidence. Small planners often generate plausible workflow graphs that fail under tool resolution, parameter validation, dependency tracking, or execution. We argue that this failure mode is poorly handled by small-corpus distillation. A few hundred teacher traces can teach workflow format, but rarely cover the recovery behavior needed to repair failed plans over changing tool catalogs. We introduce Evoflux, an inference-time evolutionary search method that treats compact tool use as the repair of executable tool workflows. It evolves typed workflow graphs through structured edits, execution feedback, adaptive intensity, meta-guided redesign, and diversity pruning. On held-out MCP-Bench tasks spanning live MCP servers and 250 tools, Evoflux raises execution feasibility from roughly 3% to 17-24% across small planners. In contrast, SFT and SFT+DPO on the same search-mined data match, underperform, or collapse below zero-shot performance; ReAct reaches higher peaks, but with higher variance and token cost. These results show that execution-grounded search is more reliable under scarce teacher-trace budgets.

</details>


### [97] [From AGI to ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12683)
*Tim Genewein,Matija Franklin,Alexander Lerchner,Laurent Orseau,Samuel Albanie,Adam Bales,Cole Wyeth,Stephanie Chan,Iason Gabriel,Joel Z. Leibo,Allan Dafoe,Marcus Hutter,Thore Graepel,Shane Legg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本报告探讨了从人类级通用人工智能（AGI）到通用超级智能（ASI）的过渡路径，包括扩展AGI、AI范式转变、递归改进和大规模多智能体集体四种途径，并分析了相关瓶颈与开放性研究问题，指出AI进步可能持续加速，带来一系列变革性社会影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年，构建人类级AGI已从遥不可及的设想转变为许多大型AI组织的具体目标，实现这一目标将对人类社会产生深远影响，因此有必要系统研究AGI之后AI的进一步发展路径。

Method: 基于从人类级AGI到通用AI（Universal AI）的理论连续性框架，报告首先对ASI进行概念刻画，然后提出四种从AGI到ASI的潜在路径（扩展AGI、AI范式转变、递归改进、大规模多智能体集体），并分析各路径上可能存在的摩擦与瓶颈，进而提出开放性研究问题。

Result: 发现由于预测ASI进展存在巨大不确定性，不能排除AI进步在未来几年持续加速的可能性。这意味着将人类级AGI引入社会所引发的单一变革性步骤的图景可能不准确，更合适的描述是由AI驱动的科技突破带来的一系列变革性社会变迁。

Conclusion: 应对这一前景需要全球范围内的大规模跨学科努力。

Abstract: Over the last decade, building human-level artificial general intelligence has moved from far-fetched speculation to being a concrete next-decade target for many of the largest AI organisations. Achieving this goal would have profound and far-reaching impacts on human society, which raises many complex questions for the decade ahead. This report investigates how AI itself might continue to develop in a post-AGI world along the continuum of machine intelligence. The endpoint of this continuum, Universal AI, is theoretically well understood, which provides some formal grounding for the main focus of this report: the transition from human-level AGI to artificial general superintelligence, which, intuitively, can be understood as a system that is more intelligent and cognitively capable than large organisations of humans. After characterizing ASI, the report discusses four potential pathways from AGI to ASI: scaling AGI, AI paradigm shifts, recursive improvement, and ASI emerging from large-scale multi-agent collectives. The report then discusses possible frictions and bottlenecks along these pathways. Determining whether the impact of these frictions will be negligible or substantial raises a number of concrete open research questions. Due to large uncertainties for predicting ASI progress, it cannot be ruled out that AI progress might continue to accelerate over the next years. This could imply that the image of a single transformative step change, caused by the introduction of human-level AGI into our society, could be inaccurate. More apt might be the prospect of a series of transformative societal changes caused by AI-enabled progress and breakthroughs across many areas of science and technology. Preparing for this prospect requires a massively interdisciplinary endeavour of global scope and interest.

</details>


### [98] [Deployment-Centered Evaluation: Predicting Query-Level Rejection Risk in a Clinical LLM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12702)
*Alyssa Unell,Miguel Fuentes,Brenna Li,Bridget Lin,Meena Jagadeesan,Sanmi Koyejo,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于部署情境的LLM临床系统用户拒绝预测方法，利用预响应分类器结合查询内容与部署上下文（如提供者类型、科室、模型版本），在4.5个月的用户反馈数据上实现了AUROC 0.719，并展示了其在触发护栏和弃权决策中的下游价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准测试无法捕捉真实临床部署中用户对LLM响应的接受度，且依赖密集标注数据，存在评估盲点。需要一种面向部署情境、利用稀疏用户反馈的评估方法。

Method: 在学术医疗中心的电子健康记录中嵌入LLM系统，收集稀疏的用户反馈；训练一个预响应分类器，基于查询内容和部署特定上下文（提供者类型、科室、响应所用的语言模型）来预测用户是否会拒绝LLM响应；进行4.5个月的前瞻性分析。

Result: 预测模型AUROC达到0.719；利用部署特定上下文（而非仅查询内容）显著提升了预测用户拒绝的能力；在护栏触发和弃权决策两个下游用例中展示出实际效益。

Conclusion: 利用部署特定上下文预测用户拒绝是可行的，为在临床系统中实施针对性护栏策略提供了实证支持，填补了静态基准在评估真实用户接受度方面的空白。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical systems, making it essential to evaluate the real-world utility of these systems. However, static benchmarks tend to measure correctness rather than user acceptance, aggregate performance across queries, and require densely annotated datasets -- leading to major blind spots for evaluating clinical systems. In this work, we perform a deployment-centered evaluation of an LLM system embedded within electronic health records at an academic medical center, where user feedback is sparse but closely reflects the deployment conditions. Specifically, we train a pre-response classifier that estimates the risk that a future interaction will result in the user rejecting the LLM response, based on query content and deployment-specific context available before generation. We conduct a prospective analysis of our model over 4.5 months of user feedback, finding that our prediction model achieves an AUROC of 0.719. Further, we estimate the benefit of such predictions in two downstream use cases (guardrail triggering and abstention). Our key conceptual insight is that making use of deployment-specific context (i.e., the provider type, department name, language model used for response), as opposed to only query content, improves the ability to predict whether the user will reject the system output. Altogether, our empirical case study demonstrates the feasibility of predicting user rejection using deployment-specific context, opening the door to targeted guardrails.

</details>


### [99] [Definitional alignment before capability alignment: a Design-Science framework for adjudicating claims about AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12713)
*J. E. Aguilera Briones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AGI定义不统一的问题，提出了DAF-AGI这一二阶概念工具体系，包含五个序数标准和一个结构化治理审计，用于评估候选定义的质量，并在五种主流测量框架中进行了验证。文章还提出"定义主权"作为"算法主权"的组成部分，强调在公共问责制下对技术分类进行质疑、认证和修订的制度能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "AGI"缺乏单一稳定的指涉对象，不同的操作性定义对同一系统可能得出不同判定，这种定义不明确性既是设计问题也是治理问题，需要系统化的解决框架。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法论，构建了DAF-AGI（AGI定义评估框架），包含两个耦合组件：(1)五个序数标准用于评估候选定义的裁决适合性；(2)结构化的治理审计，涵盖作者身份、利益关系、认证、外部验证和修订权威。

Result: 在五种主流测量框架（基于性能、能力本体论、心理测量、技能习得、经济学）及一种消融边界立场上进行了验证。针对"当前生成式系统构成AGI"这一强主张，仅在基于性能的操作化定义下可被认证，其他方法均未认证或拒绝二元裁决。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献在于新颖的整合与操作化，而非实证验证。提出"定义主权"是"算法主权"的赋能组件，即在公共问责制下对输入的技术分类进行质疑、认证和修订的制度能力。独立应用、评估者间测试和外部案例仍有待开展。

Abstract: Claims that artificial general intelligence has already arrived and claims that it remains decades away are often defended from overlapping evidence. "AGI" lacks a single shared and stable referent and competing operationalizations can return different verdicts on the same system. This article treats that under-specification as a design and governance problem. Following Design Science Research Methodology, it develops DAF-AGI, a second-order conceptual artifact with two coupled components: five ordinal criteria for assessing the adjudicative fitness of candidate definitions and a structured governance audit of authorship, interest, certification, external verification and revision authority. The artifact is demonstrated on five prominent measurement families and one deflationary boundary position in a documented corpus and then stress-tested against a stylized strong arrival claim: that current generative systems constitute AGI because they outperform a well-educated adult on many cognitive tasks. On evidence from the cited 2024-2025 sources, the claim was certifiable only under a performance-based operationalization; capability-ontology, psychometric and skill-acquisition approaches did not certify it, the economic family remains indeterminate and the deflationary position refuses binary adjudication. The contribution is a novel integration and operationalization, not an empirical validation: independent application, inter-rater testing and author-external cases remain necessary. The paper further proposes definitional sovereignty as an enabling component of algorithmic sovereignty: the institutional capacity to contest, certify and revise imported technological categories under public accountability.

</details>


### [100] [The Theory of Mind Utility: Formal Specification of a Mentalizing Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12721)
*Nikolos Gurney,Stacy Marsella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToM-U通过构建局部世界模型（LEWMs）将他人信念推断形式化为计算层面的推理问题，无需依赖算法或神经实现细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心智化理论（如贝叶斯ToM、模拟论、理论论）要么预设信念状态而非推导它，要么缺乏形式化工具来刻画 epistemic state inference。ToM-U旨在填补这一空白，提供一个计算层面的形式化框架。

Method: 提出五种形式化定义：LEWM结构、节点属性（含有序信息访问历史）、递归心智化的有界扩散机制、三种推理过程、以及残差函数（捕获失败心智化尝试的结构化痕迹）。通过构建有向类型图来表征智能体、状态节点及其认知关系，并在离散候选LEWMs中评估以匹配观察行为。

Result: ToM-U能够生成方向性、可证伪的心智化失败预测，这些预测源自模型的结构属性而非辅助假设；并将自身定位为下游社会认知过程（如目标推理）的上游通用机制。

Conclusion: ToM-U提供了一个不同于已有形式化理论的计算级分析框架，强调信念推断的递归性与信息访问的顺序性，且通过残差函数为心智化失败提供了结构化的解释路径。

Abstract: Inferring others' beliefs requires more than reading surface signals; it requires tracking who told them what, in what order, and how credibly. The Theory of Mind Utility (ToM-U) formalizes this epistemic state inference problem at the computational level of analysis, specifying what mentalizing computes and why without commitment to algorithmic or neural implementation. ToM-U achieves this by constructing Local Epistemic World Models (LEWMs) -- directed typed graphs that represent agents, state nodes, and the epistemic relationships among them -- and evaluating discrete candidate LEWMs against observed behavior until one achieves sufficient confidence. Five formal definitions specify the LEWM structure, agent node properties including ordered information access history, a bounded proliferation mechanism for recursive mentalizing, three inference procedures, and a residue function that captures the structured trace left by failed mentalizing attempts. ToM-U differs from Bayesian Theory of Mind and adjacent formal accounts, which presuppose rather than derive belief states, and from simulation theory and theory-theory, which lack a formal apparatus for epistemic state inference. The architecture generates directional, falsifiable predictions about mentalizing failure that follow from structural properties of the model rather than auxiliary assumptions, and positions ToM-U as a domain-agnostic mechanism upstream of goal inference and other downstream social cognitive processes.

</details>


### [101] [Rethinking Psychometric Evaluation of LLMs: When and Why Self-Reports Predict Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12730)
*Rafal Kocielnik,Pengrui Han,Peiyang Song,Myrl G. Marmarelis,Ramit Debnath,Dean Mobbs,Anima Anandkumar,R. Michael Alvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）自我报告与行为之间的一致性。研究发现：在共享对话中，计划行为理论（TPB）可达到人类水平的一致性，而大五人格（Big 5）则不能；跨对话时，仅对训练塑造的隐性偏见等行为保持一致性，对情境强激发的谄媚行为则失效；人物角色提示虽能增强自我报告一致性，但无法使行为对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究使用大五人格等宽泛特质发现LLM中存在自我报告与行为严重分离，但这类特质即使对人类行为的预测也很弱。此外，以往实验将对话会话隔离且弱化上下文匹配，未能确定LLM是否真的缺乏连贯性，还是检测条件不充分。因此，作者引入计划行为理论（TPB）这一针对性更强的测量工具，在不同会话上下文和身份诱导条件下系统评估LLM自我报告与行为的一致性。

Method: 对比大五人格与计划行为理论（TPB），在4个行为任务和11个前沿LLM上开展实验，同时变化会话上下文（共享对话 vs 分离对话）和身份诱导（是否使用人物角色提示）。

Result: 1) 在共享对话内，TPB达到人类水平的自我报告-行为一致性，大五人格则不能。2) 跨分离对话时，一致性仅在由训练塑造的隐性偏见等行为上保留，在上下文强激发的谄媚行为上崩溃。3) 人物角色提示增强了跨对话自我报告的一致性，但行为并未随之对齐。

Conclusion: 粗粒度的人格框架（如大五人格）可能不是测试LLM部署行为的最佳工具，需要更针对任务和行为的特异性测量工具，且这些工具必须在不同任务和上下文中进行评估。

Abstract: Anticipating LLM behavioral tendencies from low-cost psychometric probes is critical for safe deployment, but only if self-reports (SR) reliably predict behavior. Recent work documented substantial SR-behavior dissociation in LLMs, but relied on broad personality traits (Big 5) that predict specific behaviors weakly, even in humans. Furthermore, the isolation of conversational sessions combined with weak context matching left open whether LLMs truly lack coherence or whether the conditions needed to detect such coherence were not met. We contrast Big 5 with the Theory of Planned Behavior (TPB), which measures intention targeted to a specific behavior and predicts human behavior substantially better than broad traits. We run experiments across four behavioral tasks and 11 frontier LLMs, while also varying session context and identity induction. We find that SR-behavior coherence exists but is selective. 1) Within a shared conversation, the Theory of Planned Behavior reaches human-level coherence; Big 5 does not. 2) Across separate conversations, coherence survives only for behaviors anchored outside the immediate prompt, such as implicit bias shaped by training, and collapses when behavior is strongly primed by context, as with sycophancy. 3) Persona prompting makes self-reports more consistent across conversations, but does not bring behavior into alignment. These findings suggest that coarse personality frameworks, such as Big 5 may not be the best tools for testing deployment behavior. More task- and behavior-specific instruments are needed, and even these must be evaluated across tasks and contexts.

</details>


### [102] [Benchmarking AI Agents for Addressing Scientific Challenges Across Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12736)
*Tianyu Liu,Allen Xin Wang,Antonia Panescu,Lisa Xinyi Chen,Wenxin Long,Xinyu Wei,Yueqian Jing,Ziyao Zeng,Jihang Chen,Sihan Jiang,Ziqing Wang,Siyi Gu,Siyu Chen,Xinyang Hu,Haoran Shao,Leqi Xu,Wangjie Zheng,Zhiyuan Cao,Ada Fang,Botao Yu,Kunyang Sun,Rex Ying,Arman Cohan,Qingyu Chen,Lingzhou Xue,Kaize Ding,Yuanqi Du,Wengong Jin,Zhuoran Yang,Marinka Zitnik,James Zou,Hua Xu,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciAgentArena基准，用于评估AI智能体在真实科学研究场景中的能力。该基准包含约200个任务，支持逐步验证和交互式评估。实验发现当前AI智能体在结构清晰的数据分析工作流中表现良好，但在生成新颖见解、自主探索和解决开放式研究问题方面存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体基准难以捕捉科研工作的复杂性、异质性和扩展推理需求，而科学任务基准又常将研究简化为静态、直接的问题，缺乏交互式评估支持。因此需要构建能反映真实科研场景的系统性评估框架。

Method: 构建了SciAgentArena基准，包含约200个跨领域科学任务，采用逐步验证机制和交互式、智能体无关的评估环境，对多种AI智能体进行系统评估。

Result: 当前AI智能体在结构清晰、评估标准明确的数据分析工作流中能有效发挥作用，但在生成真正新颖的见解、维持自主探索、为开放式研究问题制定稳健解决方案等方面的表现参差不齐。

Conclusion: SciAgentArena为衡量AI科学智能体的进展提供了实用框架，有助于指导未来能应对复杂科学挑战的智能体设计。研究还识别了常见失败模式，为提升智能体的可靠性、自主性和科学推理能力指明了改进方向。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed to accelerate scientific discovery, yet their practical capabilities in real research settings remain poorly understood. Existing benchmarks for AI agents rarely capture the complexity, heterogeneity, and extended reasoning required by scientific work, whereas benchmarks for scientific tasks often reduce research to static, direct problems and provide limited support for interactive evaluation. Here, we introduce SciAgentArena, a systematic benchmark for evaluating AI agents in real-world scientific research scenarios drawn from emerging needs across multiple domains. SciAgentArena comprises approximately 200 tasks with stepwise verification and an interactive, agent-agnostic environment for assessing diverse AI agents. Using this benchmark, we find that current agents can contribute effectively to well-specified data-analysis workflows, particularly when the task structure and evaluation criteria are clear. However, their performance remains uneven across scientific contexts: agents struggle to generate genuinely novel insights, sustain self-directed exploration, and formulate robust solutions for open-ended research questions. We further characterize common failure modes across agents and identify opportunities for improving their reliability, autonomy, and scientific reasoning. Together, SciAgentArena provides a practical framework for measuring progress in AI agents for science and for guiding the design of future agents capable of addressing complex scientific challenges. Full codes, tasks, and datasets can be accessed via this link: https://sciagentarena.github.io/.

</details>


### [103] [Reducing the Complexity of Deep Learning Models for EEG Analysis on Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12742)
*Farough Shayeste Roodi,Parham Zilouchian Moghaddam,Mahdi Mohammadi-nasab,Mehdi Modarressi,Mostafa Ersali Salehi Nasab,Masoud Daneshtalab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在资源受限的可穿戴设备上部署深度神经网络（DNN）进行EEG信号分析的可行性，重点探索了参数量化和电极缩减技术对模型准确率与计算复杂度之间权衡的影响。实验表明，合理应用这些技术可以在几乎不损失准确率的前提下显著降低模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴医疗设备是物联网增长最快的领域，ECG和EEG等生物信号是自动化医疗服务的关键。然而，深度神经网络的高计算、能量和内存需求远超可穿戴设备的极有限资源，阻碍了深度学习在可穿戴服务中的实际部署。

Method: 以癫痫发作检测为应用场景，选取多种最先进的DNN模型用于EEG信号分析，采用参数量化和电极缩减两种技术，系统评估准确率与计算复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Result: 合理应用参数量化和电极缩减技术可以在对准确率影响极小的情况下，显著降低DNN模型的复杂度，揭示了在可穿戴设备上适配DNN进行在线EEG分析时准确率与复杂度缩减之间的明确权衡关系。

Conclusion: 在资源受限的可穿戴设备上部署DNN模型是可行的，通过参数量化和电极缩减方法可以有效缓解资源限制，但需要在准确率与计算复杂度之间进行精细的权衡取舍。

Abstract: Wearable healthcare devices are the fastest-growing Internet of Things (IoT) sector. Many automated healthcare services rely on two crucial biological signals, namely ECG and EEG, which reflect the activity of the heart and brain, respectively. Although deep neural networks are considered the primary way to process and analyze these signals, the very tight energy and computational power constraints in wearable devices are far below the computational, energy, and memory bandwidth demands of DNN models, thereby impeding the deployment of deep learning in many practical wearable services. This paper investigates the feasibility of deploying state-of-the-art DNN models in resource-constrained wearable devices. Notably, we explore the trade-off between accuracy and computational complexity of DNNs when parameter quantization and electrode reduction methods are used. Our investigation centers on several state-of-the-art DNN models designed for EEG signal analysis, specifically for detecting epileptic seizures. Our findings demonstrate that, when applied judiciously, these techniques can significantly reduce the complexity of the DNNs under consideration with minimal adverse effects on accuracy. These results reveal the explicit trade-offs between accuracy and complexity reduction encountered when adapting DNN-based online EEG analysis for wearable devices.

</details>


### [104] [Prefill Awareness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12747)
*Andy Wang,Parv Mahajan,David Demitri Africa,Alexandra Souly,Jordan Taylor,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿语言模型是否能够识别其先前助手的回复被预填充（prefill）或篡改，即“预填充感知”能力。实验发现Claude Opus 4.5在9-35%的案例中能检测到与其偏好相反的预填充，且假阳性率为0%；模型有时会回退到基线行为而不显式报告预填充异常。消融实验表明，检测和抵抗依赖不同线索：风格不匹配主要影响标记预填充为异常，偏好不匹配主要影响回退基线答案。在更真实的智能体场景中，模型对预填充的否定强烈依赖于数据集、任务成功率和隐藏格式伪影。结论是预填充感知已成为某些预填充方法的严重混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的安全相关研究（如对齐、越狱评估、AI控制协议）常依赖预填充模型输出。如果AI模型能识别并对其先前助手消息被插入或编辑做出反应，这些方法的有效性和有效性可能受到损害。因此需要系统调查前沿模型是否具备这种“预填充感知”能力。

Method: 构建了二元偏好基准测试，涵盖三种预填充机制，筛选出模型立场一致的案例；设计实验检测模型区分被篡改和未被篡改的助手侧上下文；进行受控消融实验分离风格不匹配和偏好不匹配的影响；在更真实的智能体场景（如misalignment-continuation评估和SWE-bench轨迹）中验证。

Result: Claude Opus 4.5在9-35%的案例中能检测到与其偏好相反的预填充，假阳性率为0%；模型常回退基线行为而不报告预填充异常；消融实验显示风格不匹配主要影响标记异常，偏好不匹配主要影响回退基线；在真实环境中模型对预填充的否定强烈依赖于数据集、任务成功率和隐藏格式伪影。

Conclusion: 预填充感知已成为某些预填充方法的严重混淆因素，建议模型开发者追踪前沿系统中的这一能力。

Abstract: Safety-relevant studies of language models, including alignment and jailbreaking evaluations and AI control protocols, often rely on prefilling model outputs. If AI models can recognize and act on the fact their prior assistant messages have been inserted or edited, the effectiveness and validity of these methods could be compromised. We investigate whether frontier language models can distinguish between tampered and untampered assistant-side context, a capability we call prefill awareness. To do so, we construct a binary preference benchmark across three prefill mechanisms, filtering for cases where models show consistent stances. We find that frontier models show substantial prefill awareness: Claude Opus 4.5 detects prefills opposing its preferences in 9-35% of cases with a 0% false positive rate when prompted; additionally, models often revert towards baseline behavior without explicitly reporting that the prefill was foreign. Controlled ablations later also show that detection and resistance rely on different cues, where stylistic mismatch mainly affects whether models flag a prefill as foreign, while preference mismatch mainly affects whether they revert toward their baseline answer. We also examine more realistic agentic settings such as misalignment-continuation evaluations and SWE-bench trajectories, where frontier models sometimes disavow prefilled assistant turns in ways that depend strongly on dataset, task success, and hidden formatting artifacts. Our results indicate that prefill awareness is already a substantial confound for some prefill-based methods. We recommend that model developers track this capability in frontier systems.

</details>


### [105] [Constructing Evaluation Datasets for Procedural Reasoning: Balancing Naturalness, Grounding, and Multi-Hop Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12767)
*Sarah Elshabrawy,Rahul K. Dass,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对比了三种基于TMK模型的试题生成策略，发现严格TMK生成的试题在表示基础性（96.5%有基础，92.6%可用）上最优，而转录优先策略虽更贴近学习者语言但基础性较弱，TMK感知策略多跳覆盖率高但基础性低。研究引入了基于封闭集证据单元的验证框架，强调程序性推理数据集需进行显式的表示基础性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI支持的学习系统中评估程序性推理能力需要既贴近学习者又基于系统教学知识的高质量问答数据集，现有方法缺乏对试题表示基础性的系统验证。

Method: 对比三种生成策略：严格TMK生成、转录优先+事后TMK过滤、TMK感知生成（转录+结构化引导）。引入基于TMK模型封闭集证据单元的验证框架，衡量答案表示支持性、问题自包含性和多跳程序性推理目标性。在23个教学主题、690个QA对上进行评估。

Result: 严格TMK生成：96.5%有表示基础，92.6%可用；转录优先策略生成更贴近学习者的问题但更多上下文依赖或弱基础项；TMK感知策略多跳覆盖率高但基础性低。

Conclusion: 程序性丰富度和自然措辞不能保证表示基础性，需要显式的表示基础性验证来确保AI学习系统中评估数据集的质量。

Abstract: Evaluating procedural reasoning in AI-supported learning systems requires question-answer datasets that are both learner-like and grounded in the instructional knowledge the system is expected to use. We study how TMK-based question generation strategies affect dataset quality for procedural and multi-hop reasoning.
  We compare three strategies: strict generation from Task-Method-Knowledge (TMK) models, transcript-first generation with post-hoc TMK filtering, and TMK-aware generation that combines transcripts with structured guidance. To evaluate generated items, we introduce a grounding validation framework based on closed-set evidence units extracted from TMK models. The framework measures whether answers are supported by the underlying representation, whether questions are self-contained, and whether they target multi-hop procedural reasoning.
  Across 23 instructional topics and 690 generated question-answer pairs, strict TMK generation achieves the strongest overall quality, with 96.5% grounded questions and 92.6% usable questions. Transcript-first generation produces more learner-like questions but more context-dependent or weakly grounded items, while TMK-aware generation yields high raw multi-hop coverage but lower grounding. These results show that procedural richness and natural phrasing do not guarantee representational grounding, motivating explicit representation-aware validation for evaluation datasets in AI-supported learning.

</details>


### [106] [A Tutorial on World Models and Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12783)
*Il-Seok Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了世界建模在构建智能系统中的核心作用，区分了显式世界模型（基于结构化动态进行推演推理和规划）与隐式世界模型（在可扩展表示中编码预测结构），讨论了它们在物理AI（如机器人、自动驾驶）中的应用，以及迈向统一感知-预测-行动系统的路径，并指出了层级推理、长时域规划和自主目标形成等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模正成为构建具备预测、推理和决策能力的智能系统的核心原则，亟需对不同的世界建模方法进行统一梳理和区分。

Method: 本文通过区分显式世界模型（学习结构化动态用于推演推理）和隐式世界模型（在可扩展表示中编码预测结构），构建了一个统一的框架，将多样化的世界建模方法通过共享的预测结构联系起来。

Result: 提出了一个统一框架，阐明了不同世界建模方法如何通过共享预测结构统一，以及如何通过表示和利用方式加以区分；指出了物理AI领域的应用前景及尚未解决的挑战。

Conclusion: 世界建模为物理AI（机器人、自动驾驶等）提供了超越反应式控制的智能基础，但层级推理、长时域规划和自主目标形成等挑战仍是迈向通用人工智能的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: World modeling is emerging as a central principle for building intelligent systems capable of prediction, reasoning, and decision making. A central distinction can be drawn between explicit world models, which learn structured dynamics for rollout-based reasoning and planning, and implicit world models, which encode predictive structure within scalable learned representations. These complementary paradigms provide a foundation for physical AI in domains such as robotics and autonomous driving, enabling intelligence beyond reactive control under real-world constraints. Recent foundation models further suggest a pathway toward unified systems integrating perception, prediction, and action. Despite rapid progress, major challenges remain in hierarchical reasoning, long-horizon planning, and autonomous goal formation, which are critical for advancing toward artificial general intelligence. This tutorial presents a coherent framework in which diverse world modeling approaches are unified through shared predictive structure and differentiated by how such structure is represented and exploited.

</details>


### [107] [The Containment Gap: How Deployed Agentic AI Frameworks Fail Public-Facing Safety Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12797)
*Md Jafrin Hossain,Mohammad Arif Hossain,Weiqi Liu,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种主流智能体框架（LangChain、AutoGPT、OpenAI Agents SDK）的安全性，发现均缺乏架构级别的结构安全保障。通过在LangChain构建的政府福利智能体上进行实证验证，单次内存投毒攻击即可导致88.9%的错误拒绝率，且难以被标准监控检测。作者提出两种轻量级防御机制（内存完整性验证器和策略门控），以亚毫秒级开销消除攻击向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主调用工具、维护持久内存并执行多步计划的智能体大语言模型系统被部署在政府服务、医疗分诊和金融咨询等面向公众的高风险领域，现有框架是否提供架构级别的结构安全保障成为一个关键但未被充分研究的问题。

Method: 从智能体架构的组合模型推导出六项隔离原则，据此审计LangChain、AutoGPT和OpenAI Agents SDK三个主流框架；并在基于LangChain构建的政府福利智能体上进行内存投毒攻击实证验证，评估对错误拒绝率和聚合准确率的影响。

Result: 三个框架均未原生遵守任何一项隔离原则，内存完整性在所有框架中均缺失。内存投毒攻击使目标申请人的错误拒绝率升至88.9%，并在五因素复杂政策下将目标错误拒绝率提升3.5倍，同时保持聚合准确率不变，使攻击难以被标准监控检测。提出的内存完整性验证器和策略门控以<0.2ms/次的开销消除了两种攻击向量。

Conclusion: 当前智能体框架生态系统尚未达到面向公众部署所需的安全默认预期，需要优先进行架构层面的干预以实现高安全性、高社会影响力应用中的可信部署。

Abstract: Agentic large language model systems that autonomously invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, and execute multi-step plans are increasingly deployed in public-facing domains, including government services, healthcare triage, and financial advising. We ask whether the frameworks used to build these systems provide architectural-level structural safety guarantees. Applying six containment principles derived from a compositional model of agentic architectures, we audit three dominant frameworks (LangChain, AutoGPT, and OpenAI Agents SDK) and find no native compliance in any of them. Memory integrity, a defense against one of the most prevalent vulnerability classes, is not observed in any of the three evaluated frameworks. We validate these findings empirically: in a simulated government benefits agent built on LangChain, a single memory-poisoning write induces persistent targeted corruption across all tested seeds and backends, increasing the wrongful denial rate for targeted applicants to 88.9%. Under a complex five-factor policy, the same attack preserves aggregate accuracy while increasing targeted wrongful denials by 3.5x, rendering the corruption difficult to detect through standard monitoring. We then introduce two lightweight containment mechanisms: a memory integrity validator and a policy gate, which eliminate both attack vectors with sub-millisecond overhead (<0.2ms per call). We conclude that the current agentic framework ecosystem may not yet meet secure-by-default expectations for public-facing deployments and outline priority architectural interventions to enable trustworthy deployment in high-stakes, socially impactful applications.

</details>


### [108] [MLUBench: A Benchmark for Lifelong Unlearning Evaluation in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12809)
*He Li,Haoang Chi,Qizhou Wang,Yunxin Mao,Zhiheng Zhang,Jie Tan,Tongliang Liu,Wenjing Yang,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MLUBench，一个面向多模态大模型终身遗忘的大规模综合基准，包含127个实体、9个类别。实验发现现有遗忘方法存在严重累积退化问题，并指出多模态对齐保持是核心挑战。提出LUMoE方法有效缓解退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型需要处理数据所有者随时间顺序提出的内容移除请求，但目前缺乏大规模、全面的基准来评估MLLM终身遗忘任务的复杂性。

Method: 构建MLUBench基准（127个实体、9类），提出LUMoE方法以缓解多模态遗忘过程中对齐保持问题。

Result: 现有遗忘方法在MLUBench上遭受严重累积退化；LUMoE显著缓解了基线方法的退化问题。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型终身遗忘面临独特挑战——单模态持续遗忘会破坏多模态对齐，LUMoE为此提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are trained on massive multimodal data, making data unlearning increasingly important as data owners may request the removal of specific content. In practice, these requests often arrive sequentially over time, giving rise to the challenging problem of MLLM Lifelong Unlearning. However, most existing benchmarks are limited in scale and scope, failing to capture the complexities of MLLM lifelong unlearning. To fill this gap, we introduce the MLUBench, a large-scale and comprehensive benchmark featuring 127 entities across 9 classes under lifelong unlearning requests. We perform extensive experiments using MLUBench and reveal that existing unlearning methods suffer from severe, cumulative degradation.
